Detailed algorithms:

1. Multiplicative/Projected mixed update (MIX):
Inputs p (np array length n, sum1), time budget.
- Maintain p_list? no.
- Parameters: beta initial 2, increase slowly; eta initial maybe 0.1; noise.
- Iterate while time left and no stagnation:
   c=convolve(p)
   M=max(c)
   ensure p finite, sum>0.
   grad_log = p * d where d=sliding_sum_y_p(y,p) with y=softmax(beta*(c-M)/M?)
     Use y = exp(beta * (c / M -1)) maybe so max active weight 1, others decay. Normalize y sum=1 to stabilize. Could also y=c/(sum c)? But y sum arbitrary; direction scale controlled by eta. Use y weights sum=1 for natural gradient: log update p_i←p_i exp(-eta * weighted contribution). If y sum=1, contribution g_i=∑ y_k p_i p_{k-i}; sum g =∑ y c. Entropy. Use eta scaled 1/(sum g). 
   d_i =∑ y_k p_{k-i}; can compute via conv y,p[::-1]. Then g=p*d. Since p normalized, ∑g=∑ y c ≈ M_active? For uniform central M~1/n, ∑c=1, ∑y c maybe M? If y concentrated, ~M. Then eta ~ 1/M maybe large. We can choose step by targeting relative change: let G = ∑g (positive). eta = α / G (α ~0.1-1) ensures ∑ log changes? Then p_new_i = p_i * exp(-eta (g_i - G/n))? Center to preserve sum roughly. Or use p_new = p_i * exp(-eta*(g_i - mean_g)); normalize.
   Add noise: p_new += noise * sigma * p? or log noise.
   Line search: try t in [0,0.1,0.25,0.5,0.7,1.0]; p_alpha = (1-t)*p + t*p_new; normalize; score. Since score may be nonconvex, maybe choose best; if none better than current by small, reject? To allow escaping, simulated annealing: accept if worse by <accept_window, with temperature decreasing. But prioritize monotone.
   Update beta: increase every stagnation.
- Periodically reset to best plus random.

This is heuristic. Could get stuck at symmetric local minimum if start symmetric; start with asymmetry.

Let's analyze if finite-n target 1.5027 likely needs n >=? The score lower bound maybe 2/(1+1/n?)? Let's derive universal lower bound to know minimal n. For any p, central coefficient c_{n-1}=∑ p_i p_{n-1-i}. What lower bound? Let S_i = p_i+p_{n-1-i} for pairs. Pair product p_i p_{n-1-i} ≥ S_i²/4. Let total mass in left halves. For n odd, maybe. Implementation details for A building in LP:
def build_convolution_matrix(p):
 n=len(p); A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
 for j in range(n):
    A[j:j+n, j] = p
 return A
This creates columns as p shifted down. For row k, A[k,j]=p[k-j] if k-j between0,n-1. For j, rows k=j to j+n-1 contain p[0..n-1]. Yes. Size (2n-1,n). For n=1024, 2M entries.

Multiplicative step details implemented robust:
def multiplicative_step(p, c=None, beta=10.0, step=0.2, noise=0.0, rng=None):
   if c is None: c=conv(p); M=max(c)
   # y weighted by c/M: y = np.exp(beta*(c - M)) / sum? For c near M weight 1.
   y = np.exp(beta*(c - M)) # max1; underflow okay
   if sum y <=0: uniform
   y /= sum y
   # d_i = ∑ y_k p_{k-i} via conv
   y_ext = y; p_rev = p[::-1]
   tmp = np.convolve(y, p_rev)  # length 3n-2. But y length 2n-1, p_rev n. d_i = tmp[n-1-i]
   d = tmp[n-1-(np.arange(n))]  # index max n-1 down? For i=0 index n-1, i=n-1 index0. Actually earlier d_i=tmp[n-1-i]. So d_rev = tmp[:n][::-1]? tmp length 3n-2, indices 0..3n-3. n-1-i ranges 0..n-1. This is first n reversed? For i=0 -> n-1; i=n-1->0. So d = tmp[:n][::-1]. Check: tmp[r]=∑ y_k p_rev_{r-k}. For r=n-1-i. d_i as above.
   # Alternative use correlate(y,p,'valid') but conv okay.
   g = p*d
   mean_g = g.mean()
   logfac = -step*(g-mean_g)
   # maybe bound to avoid overflow: logfac clipped ±5
   p2 = p*np.exp(np.clip(logfac, -5,5))
   if noise>0: p2 *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, noise, n).clip(-2,2))
   p2 = np.clip(p2, 0, None); s=p2.sum(); if s>0 p2/=s
   return p2

Implement linear_conv(a,b):
```
def linear_conv(a,b):
    la, lb = len(a), len(b)
    if la == 0 or lb ==0: return np.array([])
    if min(la,lb) < 64 or (la+lb) < 2048:
        return np.convolve(a,b)
    m = 1
    need = la + lb -1
    while m < need: m <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b,m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, m)
    return c[:need]
```
For square: use fa*fa.

For nonnegative p, FFT convolution maybe slight negative; score uses max(c). Could use c = np.where(c<0,0,c) if small. But if actual negative impossible. Use c = np.maximum(c, 0.0) for max? For internal max, negative not issue. For y = exp(beta(c-M)), negative c -> exp underflow; okay.

Line search alpha:
```
def line_search(p, p2, base_score):
   best = base_score; best_p = p
   for alpha in [1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4,0.3,0.2,0.12,0.06,0.03,0.01]:
      c = p + alpha*(p2-p); normalize
      sc, cconv = score_p(c)
      if sc < best: best=sc; best_p=c
   return best_p,best_score
```

Define p from Fourier:
```
def fourier_to_p(theta, n, max_AMP=1.5):
   # theta pairs (amp, phase)
   t = np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
   val = np.ones(n)
   for m, (A, ph) in enumerate(theta.reshape(-1,2), start=1):
       val += A*np.cos(2π*m*t/n + ph)
   # ensure positive by adding max(0, 1-min?) or using exp
   # Better: log_p = sum A cos + constant; normalize exp
```

        p,score,improved = line_search(..., p2, score, accept_temp=...)
        if improved: no_improve=0 else decrement etc.

```
def safe_linprog_max(A,b,ub=50.0):
    c = -np.ones(A.shape[1])
    try:
       res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, ub), method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-7,'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-7})
       if res.success and res.x is not None: return np.asarray(res.x)
    except Exception:
       pass
    return None
```

Detailed helpers:

`_normalize_positive(x)`:
```
def _normalize_positive(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    x[x < 0] = 0.0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
       x = np.ones_like(x)
    x = x + 1e-12
    s = x.sum()
    if s <= 0: return np.ones(len(x))/len(x)
    return x/s
```
But adding eps changes sum; divide after.

`_linear_conv(a,b)`:
```
def _linear_conv(a,b=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); 
    if b is None: b=a
    need=len(a)+len(b)-1
    if len(a)==0 or len(b)==0: return np.zeros(0)
    if need <= 3000 or min(len(a), len(b)) <= 64:
       return np.convolve(a,b)
    m=1
    while m<need: m*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,m); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,m)
    return c[:need]
```
For square, `fb=fa`.
Potential negative small: in score, `c = np.maximum(c,0.0)`. But in multiplicative y, negative not. Use c_clean.

`_score_p(p)`:
```
def _score_p(p):
   p = np.asarray(p,float)
   n=len(p)
   c = _linear_conv(p,p)
   c = c[:2*n-1]
   c = np.where(c<0,0.0,c)
   M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   s = float(p.sum())
   # but p should sum1; to be safe: score = 2*n*max(rawconv with p? If p sum not1, score =2n max(p*p)/(sum p)^2)
```
If p not exactly sum1, score formula: max raw conv / s² *2n. But we normalize. For generic:
```
s = p.sum(); if s <=0: return inf,...
M = max(_linear_conv(p,p))
return 2*n*M/(s*s), clean c
```
But if we call score on p sum1, OK. For line search, normalize before.

`_line_search(p,p2,base_score)`:
```
def _line_search(p,p2,base_score, alphas=None):
    p = _normalize_positive(p); p2=_normalize_positive(p2)
    best_p=p; best_s=base_score
    for a in alphas:
       cand = (1-a)*p + a*p2
       cand = _normalize_positive(cand)
       sc, c = _score_p(cand)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s:
           best_s=sc; best_p=cand
    return best_p,best_s
```

`_smoothed_noise`:
```
def _smoothed_noise(rng,n,sigma):
   if sigma<=0 or n<=1: return np.zeros(n)
   x=rng.normal(size=n)
   m=1
   while m<2*n: m*=2
   f=np.fft.rfft(x,m)
   freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(m)*2*np.pi
   low = 1.0 + 5.0/n? Actually want low-pass. Use f *= np.exp(-freq*freq/var) maybe.
   f *= np.exp(-(freq/(2*np.pi/m* 3.0))**2)
   y=np.fft.irfft(f,m,n)
   y -= y.mean(); return y*sigma
```

def _perturb_p(p, rng, strength):
   n=len(p)
   p2=p.copy()
   width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//64), max(2,n//8)))
   start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
   p2[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,strength))
   # global lowfreq noise optional
   p2 = _normalize_positive(p2)
   return p2
```

`_lp_direction(p, M, active_count, ub)`:
```
def _lp_direction(p, M, active_count, ub=20.0, time_limit? none):
   p = _normalize_positive(p); n=len(p)
   c = _square_conv(p); M = max(M, max(c), tiny)
   rows_count=2*n-1
   full_limit = min(rows_count, 1200?)? We'll select all if rows_count<=MAX_FULL_ROWS else top active_count.
   if rows_count <= MAX_FULL_ROWS: rows=np.arange(rows_count)
   else: max_rows=min(rows_count, active_count); rows=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:max_rows]
   A=_build_active_matrix(p, rows)
   b = M * float(n)
   obj=-np.ones(n)
   z=None
   if HAS_LINPROG:
      try:
         res=linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, ub), method='highs')
         if res.success and res.x is not None: z=np.asarray(res.x,float)
      except Exception:
         z=None
   if z is None or z.sum()<=0:
       # fallback: use dual active heuristic
       return None
   z=np.clip(z,0,None)
   g=z/np.sum(z)
   return g
```
But for active rows with b=M*n and p scaled? If we selected active rows only, the LP may choose z with sum huge on columns not in active rows? Variables bounded UB=20. If p positive, constraints couple. Good.
Fallback heuristic if LP fails: `return _multiplicative_step(p, c, beta= max(5,?), step=0.5)`.

`_build_active_matrix`:
Use p maybe smoothed? Use `p` as given plus eps to ensure rows not zero? If we add eps to p, b unchanged? Fine. Use p_mat = p + EPS_POS/n? Need sum? Not critical. Just p_mat = np.clip(p, 1e-15, None).
```
def _build_active_matrix(p, rows):
    n=len(p); K=len(rows); A=np.zeros((K,n),float)
    for idx in range(K):
       k=int(rows[idx])
       jstart = k - (n-1)
       if jstart < 0: jstart=0
       jend = k
       if jend >= n: jend = n-1
       if jend < jstart: continue
       i0 = k - jend
       i1 = k - jstart
       A[idx, jstart:jend+1] = p[i0:i1+1][::-1]
    return A
```
This builds for each row. If K large, okay.

Potential performance: In LP active with n=2048,K=2048, building A maybe 2M slice copies. Fine.

def _resample_p(p, m):
   n=len(p)
   if n==m: return p
   # if n > m: block average
   if n > m:

Need `_smoothed_noise` robust. Use FFT:
```
def _smoothed_noise(rng,n,sigma):
    if sigma <=0: zeros
    m=1
    while m<2*n: m*=2
    x=rng.normal(size=n)
    f=np.fft.rfft(x,m)
    freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(m)
    # low-pass keep first 3 cycles
    cut = 3.0 / m
    f *= np.exp(-(np.abs(freq)/cut)**2)
    y=np.fft.irfft(f,m,n)
    y -= y.mean()
    return y*sigma
```
This returns smooth noise.

def _initial_set(n, seed, hs_arr=None):
   ps=[]
   rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + n*131)
   ps.append(np.ones(n)/n)
   # linear ramp
   for lam in [0.5,1.0,2.0]:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      p=np.exp(-lam*x); ps.append(_normalize_positive(p))
      p=np.exp(-lam*(1-x)); ps.append(...)
   # sine
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
   for A in [0.3,0.6,0.9]:
       for phase in [0, np.pi/2, np.pi, 3*np.pi/2]:
           val=1 + A*np.sin(2*np.pi*t + phase)
           if np.min(val)>0: ps.append(_normalize_positive(val))
   # squared sine
       val=1 + A*np.cos(2*np.pi*t + phase)
   # random smooth
   for k in range(4):
       z=_smoothed_noise(rng,n,sigma=0.7)
       ps.append(_normalize_positive(np.exp(z)))
   # block random
   for k in range(2):
       p=np.ones(n)
       width=max(1,n//50)
       while blocks<5: start... p*=exp(N)
       ps.append(...)
   # height sequence
   if hs_arr is not None and len(hs_arr)>0:
       ps.append(_resample_p(hs_arr,n))
   # deduplicate? not necessary.
   return ps
```

def _linear_conv(a,b=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); 
   if b is None:
      b=a
   else: b=np.asarray(b,float)
   ...
   if direct: return np.convolve(a,b)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
   if b is a: fb=fa
   else: fb=np.fft.rfft(b,m)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,m)
```

`_normalize_positive` adding eps inside changes mass; if p has very large, fine. Use small `1e-300` not 1e-12? For p length 4096, adding 1e-12 changes mass by 4e-9 negligible. But if p has zeros and some values large, OK. To preserve exact nonneg sequence, add `1e-15`. We also want p strictly positive for LP; 1e-15/n may underflow? fine.
```
def _normalize_positive(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float).copy()
    if x.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    x[~np.isfinite(x)] = 0.0
    x[x<0]=0.0
    x += 1e-16
    s=x.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return np.ones(x.size)/x.size
    x /= s
    return x
```
But adding 1e-16 and dividing may make sum exactly1? Floating yes.

For output, values ~1e-16 maybe positive; fine. If evaluator max cap 1000; fine.

But if n small, uniform p=1/n; adding 1e-16 shifts. fine.

```
def _square_conv(p):
    n=len(p)
    c=_linear_conv(p)
    if c.size > 2*n-1: c=c[:2*n-1]
    # fix small negative
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c
```

Let's write `_improve_sequence` in detail:
```
def _improve_sequence(p, deadline, seed, active_count=1600):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).copy()
    p=_normalize_positive(p)
    n=len(p)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    curr_score, c = _score_p_with_c(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=curr_score
    beta=5.0; step=0.25; no_improve=0; it=0
    lp_wait = 2.0 + 0.001*n # maybe
    last_lp = time.monotonic() - lp_wait - 1.0
    accept_temp = 2e-4
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it += 1
       if it % 10 == 0 and time.monotonic()-deadline?
       if time.monotonic() - last_lp >= lp_wait:
           last_lp = time.monotonic()
           g = _lp_direction(p, float(np.max(c)), active_count)
           if g is not None:
               g = _normalize_positive(0.9*g + 0.1*p)  # ensure support
               # maybe scale to sum1
               new_p, new_s = _line_search(p, g, curr_score)
               if new_s < curr_score - 1e-12:
                   p = new_p; curr_score = new_s; no_improve=0
                   if curr_score < best_score: best_score=curr_score; best_p=p.copy()
                   beta = max(1.0, beta*0.9)
                   lp_wait = max(0.8, lp_wait*0.9)
               else:
                   no_improve += 1
                   beta = min(50.0, beta*1.08)
                   lp_wait = min(20.0, lp_wait*1.15)
           else:
               no_improve +=1
           c = _square_conv(p)
       else:
           p2 = _multiplicative_step(p, c, beta, step, rng, noise=0.0 if no_improve<20 else 0.02)
           new_p, new_s = _line_search(p, p2, curr_score)
           # accept if improve or small worse with prob
           accept = False
           if new_s < curr_score - 1e-12:
               accept = True
           elif accept_temp > 1e-7 and new_s < curr_score + accept_temp:
               # greedy anneal: accept if random less than accept_temp/(new_s-curr_score)? simple
               if rng.random() < 0.2:
                   accept = True
           if accept:
               p = new_p; curr_score = new_s; no_improve=0; accept_temp = max(1e-7, accept_temp*0.97)
               if curr_score < best_score:
                   best_score=curr_score; best_p=p.copy()
           else:
               no_improve +=1
               beta = min(80.0, beta*1.04)
               step = min(0.8, step*1.02)
               accept_temp = max(1e-7, accept_temp*0.95)
           if it % 5 ==0:
               c = _square_conv(p)
       if no_improve > 80:
           p = _perturb_p(best_p, rng, strength=0.15)
           curr_score,c = _score_p_with_c(p)
           if curr_score >= best_score:
               p=best_p.copy(); curr_score=best_score; c=_square_conv(p)
           no_improve=0; beta=8.0; step=0.3; accept_temp=5e-4
    return best_p, best_score
```

if accept: c=_square_conv(p)
```

Let's write `_smoothed_noise`:
```
def _smoothed_noise(rng,n,sigma):
    if sigma <=0: return zeros
    m=1
    while m < 2*n: m <<=1
    x=rng.normal(size=n)
    f=np.fft.rfft(x,m)
    f *= np.exp(-2.0 * np.abs(np.fft.rfftfreq(m)) * np.log(2.0) * 6.0) ?
```
Simpler: keep only first 4 nonzero frequencies:
```
freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(m)
mask = (np.abs(freq) <= 4.0/m)
f[~mask]=0.0
y = np.fft.irfft(f,m,n)
```
This yields very smooth. For n large, first 4 modes. Good. Maybe need more modes; use cutoff=8/m.
Scale to sigma relative std. If y std=0, return zeros.
```
std = y.std()
if std>0: y = y/std * sigma
else zeros
```
Good.

`_perturb_p`:
```
def _perturb_p(p, rng, strength=0.15):
    p=_normalize_positive(p); n=len(p)
    p2=p.copy()
    if strength>0:
       z=_smoothed_noise(rng,n,strength*0.5)
       p2 *= np.exp(z)
       # a few blocks
       nblocks=int(rng.integers(1,4))
       for _ in range(nblocks):
          width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//128), max(2,n//16)))
          start=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-width)))
          p2[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0,strength*0.5)))
    return _normalize_positive(p2)
```
No nested loops? ok.

`_resample_p`:
```
def _resample_p(p,m):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.ones(m)/m
    if n==m: return _normalize_positive(p)
    if m<=1: return np.array([1.0])
    xp=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    xq=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    y=np.interp(xq,xp,p)
    return _normalize_positive(y)
```
This works for up/down. If hs raw not normalized, _normalize.

Let's consider if `linprog` is imported but method highs not available. `linprog(..., method='highs')` may raise ValueError. We can catch and maybe try default without method. Implement in `_safe_linprog`:
```
def _safe_linprog(A,b,ub):
   if not HAS_LINPROG: return None
   c=-np.ones(A.shape[1])
   try:
      res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, ub), method='highs')
   except Exception:
      try: res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, ub))
      except Exception: return None
   if res.success: return np.asarray(res.x,float)
   return None
```
If method highs not available, default may be 'simplex' or 'interior-point'; okay.

If `linprog` solution has small negative values, clip.

```python
"""
Search for low-score nonnegative step functions.

The score is scale invariant: for normalized p (sum 1),
score = 2*n*max_k (p*p)_k, where p*p is the full linear convolution.
The main search combines:
  1) fast mirror-descent / multiplicative updates driven by a softened max,
  2) the LP direction from the previous state-of-the-art:
     maximize sum(g) subject to p*g <= M on active constraints,
  3) line search and random resets to avoid local plateaus.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    _HAS_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    linprog = None
    _HAS_LINPROG = False

_FULL_ROWS_LIMIT = 2048
_LP_UB = 50.0
_LP_MIX = 0.10
_ALPHAS_LP = (1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25, 0.15, 0.08, 0.04)
_ALPHAS_CHEAP = (0.8, 0.5, 0.3, 0.15, 0.07)
_TARGET = 1.5027


def _normalize_positive(x):
    """Clip to nonnegative, force finite, normalize to sum 1."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    x[~np.isfinite(x)] = 0.0
    x[x < 0.0] = 0.0
    x += 1e-16
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
    x /= s
    return x


def _linear_conv(a, b=None):
    """Full linear convolution with direct/FFT choice."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if b is None:
        b = a
    else:
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)

    la = int(a.size)
    lb = int(b.size)
    if la == 0 or lb == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = la + lb - 1

    if need <= 2048 or min(la, lb) <= 32:
        return np.convolve(a, b).astype(np.float64, copy=False)

    m = 1
    while m < need:
        m <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    if b is a:
        fb = fa
    else:
        fb = np.fft.rfft(b, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, m)
    c = c[:need]
    return c.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _square_conv(p):
    """Return cleaned full coefficients of P(z)^2."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    c = _linear_conv(p, p)
    if c.size > 2 * n - 1:
        c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = c.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def _score_p_with_c(p):
    """Score and convolution coefficients for p."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    c = _square_conv(p)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf, c
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * M / (s * s)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = np.inf
    return score, c


def _line_search(p, p2, base_score, alphas=None):
    """Best convex combination (1-alpha)p + alpha p2 by score."""
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    p2 = _normalize_positive(p2)
    if alphas is None:
        alphas = _ALPHAS_CHEAP

    best_p = p
    best_s = float(base_score)
    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        best_s = np.inf

    for alpha in alphas:
        alpha = float(alpha)
        if alpha <= 0.0 or alpha > 1.0:
            continue
        cand = (1.0 - alpha) * p + alpha * p2
        cand = _normalize_positive(cand)
        sc, _ = _score_p_with_c(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s:
            best_s = sc
            best_p = cand

    return best_p, float(best_s)


def _smoothed_noise(rng, n, sigma):
    """Low-frequency Gaussian-like noise, standardized, multiplied by sigma."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1 or sigma <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    m = 1
    while m < 2 * n:
        m <<= 1

    x = rng.normal(size=n)
    f = np.fft.rfft(x, m)
    freq = np.abs(np.fft.rfftfreq(m))
    cut = 8.0 / float(m)
    f *= np.exp(-(freq / cut) ** 2)
    y = np.fft.irfft(f, m, n)

    mean = float(np.mean(y))
    y -= mean
    std = float(np.std(y))
    if std > 1e-15:
        y *= sigma / std
    else:
        y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return y


def _perturb_p(p, rng, strength=0.15):
    """Random smooth and blockwise multiplicative perturbation."""
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    p2 = p.copy()

    if strength > 0.0:
        z = _smoothed_noise(rng, n, 0.5 * float(strength))
        p2 *= np.exp(z)

        nblocks = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        for _ in range(nblocks):
            low_w = max(1, n // 128)
            high_w = max(low_w + 1, n // 16)
            width = int(rng.integers(low_w, high_w))
            if width >= n:
                width = max(1, n // 2)
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width)))
            factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5 * float(strength))))
            p2[start:start + width] *= factor

    return _normalize_positive(p2)


def _multiplicative_step(p, c=None, beta=5.0, step=0.3, rng=None, noise=0.0):
    """
    One mirror-descent style step.

    c_k = (p*p)_k.
    y_k = exp(beta * (c_k/M - 1)) is a dual weight.
    g_i = p_i * sum_k y_k p_{k-i}.
    p_i <- p_i exp(step_centered(g)_i), renormalized.
    """
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if c is None:
        c = _square_conv(p)

    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return p

    z = c / M
    y = np.exp(float(beta) * (z - 1.0))
    ys = float(np.sum(y))
    if ys <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ys):
        y = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        ys = 1.0
    y /= ys

    # d_i = sum_k y_k p_{k-i}
    tmp = _linear_conv(y, p[::-1])
    if tmp.size < n:
        d = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        d = tmp[:n][::-1].copy()
    d[~np.isfinite(d)] = 0.0
    d[d < 0.0] = 0.0

    g = p * d
    g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
    g_mean = float(np.mean(g))
    g_scale = float(np.max(g))
    if g_scale <= 1e-300:
        g_scale = 1e-300

    logfac = -float(step) * (g - g_mean) / g_scale

    if rng is not None and abs(float(noise)) > 0.0:
        # small smooth noise for escape
        z = _smoothed_noise(rng, n, abs(float(noise)))
        logfac += z

    logfac = np.clip(logfac, -8.0, 8.0)
    p2 = p * np.exp(logfac)
    return _normalize_positive(p2)


def _build_active_matrix(p, rows):
    """Build dense matrix A for selected convolution rows k: A[k,j]=p[k-j]."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    K = int(np.size(rows))
    A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)

    for idx in range(K):
        k = int(rows[idx])
        if k < 0 or k > 2 * n - 2:
            continue

        jstart = k - (n - 1)
        if jstart < 0:
            jstart = 0
        jend = k
        if jend >= n:
            jend = n - 1
        if jend < jstart:
            continue

        i0 = k - jend
        i1 = k - jstart
        A[idx, jstart:jend + 1] = p[i0:i1 + 1][::-1]

    return A


def _safe_linprog(A, b, ub):
    """_wrap linprog max sum(x) s.t. A x <= b, 0<=x<=ub._"""
    if not _HAS_LINPROG:
        return None
    if A.size == 0:
        return None

    c_obj = -np.ones(A.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, float(ub)), method="highs")
        if getattr(res, "success", False) and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            x[~np.isfinite(x)] = 0.0
            return np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, float(ub)))
        if getattr(res, "success", False) and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            x[~np.isfinite(x)] = 0.0
            return np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return None


def _lp_direction(p, M, active_count=1600):
    """
    Approximate the SOTA LP direction:
      maximize sum(z) s.t. A_selected z <= b, 0<=z<=UB,
    where z = n*g and p is normalized. Returns normalized g or None.
    """
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    c = _square_conv(p)
    rows_count = int(c.size)
    if rows_count == 0:
        return None
    M = float(M) if M is not None else float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return None

    max_rows = rows_count if rows_count <= _FULL_ROWS_LIMIT else min(rows_count, int(active_count))
    max_rows = max(8, min(max_rows, rows_count))

    if rows_count <= max_rows and rows_count <= _FULL_ROWS_LIMIT:
        rows = np.arange(rows_count, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        threshold = 0.45 * M
        cand = np.where(c >= threshold)[0]
        if cand.size > max_rows:
            order = np.argsort(-c[cand])
            rows = cand[order[:max_rows]]
        else:
            need = max(0, max_rows - int(cand.size))
            if need > 0:
                top_all = np.argsort(-c)[:need]
                rows = np.unique(np.concatenate((cand, top_all)))
            else:
                rows = cand
            if rows.size > max_rows:
                rows = rows[:max_rows]

    if rows.size == 0:
        return None

    c_rows = c[rows]
    b = float(n) * ((1.0 - _LP_MIX) * c_rows + _LP_MIX * M) + float(n) * 1e-12

    A = _build_active_matrix(p, rows)
    z = _safe_linprog(A, b, _LP_UB)
    if z is None:
        return None

    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z[~np.isfinite(z)] = 0.0
    z = np.clip(z, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(z))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None

    g = z / s
    g = _normalize_positive(g)
    return g


def _resample_p(p, m):
    """Resample a normalized sequence to length m."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0 or n == 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    if n == m:
        return _normalize_positive(p)

    xp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    xq = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    y = np.interp(xq, xp, p)
    return _normalize_positive(y)


def _smoothed_noise(rng, n, sigma):
    """Low-frequency Gaussian-like noise, standardized, multiplied by sigma."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1 or sigma <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    m = 1
    while m < 2 * n:
        m <<= 1

    x = rng.normal(size=n)
    f = np.fft.rfft(x, m)
    freq = np.abs(np.fft.rfftfreq(m))
    cut = 8.0 / float(m)
    f *= np.exp(-(freq / cut) ** 2)
    y = np.fft.irfft(f, m, n)

    y -= np.mean(y)
    std = float(np.std(y))
    if std > 1e-15:
        y *= sigma / std
    else:
        y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return y


def _perturb_p(p, rng, strength=0.15):
    """Random smooth and blockwise multiplicative perturbation."""
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    p2 = p.copy()

    if strength > 0.0:
        z = _smoothed_noise(rng, n, 0.5 * float(strength))
        p2 *= np.exp(z)

        nblocks = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        for _ in range(nblocks):
            low_w = max(1, n // 128)
            high_w = max(low_w + 1, n // 16)
            width = int(rng.integers(low_w, high_w))
            if width >= n:
                width = max(1, n // 2)
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width)))
            factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5 * float(strength))))
            p2[start:start + width] *= factor

    return _normalize_positive(p2)


def _initial_set(n, seed, hs_arr=None):
    """Generate varied nonnegative normalized starting sequences."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return [np.array([1.0])]

    ps = []
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 1000003 * n)

    ps.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    for lam in (0.5, 1.0, 1.6, 2.5, 4.0):
        ps.append(_normalize_positive(np.exp(-float(lam) * x)))
        ps.append(_normalize_positive(np.exp(-float(lam) * (1.0 - x))))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    for A in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.95):
        for phase in (0.0, 0.5 * np.pi, np.pi, 1.5 * np.pi):
            val1 = 1.0 + A * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + phase)
            if np.min(val1) > 0.0:
                ps.append(_normalize_positive(val1))
            val2 = 1.0 + A * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t + phase)
            if np.min(val2) > 0.0:
                ps.append(_normalize_positive(val2))

    for k in range(5):
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 0.9))
        z = _smoothed_noise(rng, n, sigma)
        ps.append(_normalize_positive(np.exp(z)))

    for k in range(2):
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        nblocks = int(rng.integers(3, 8))
        for _ in range(nblocks):
            width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 128), max(2, n // 16)))
            if width >= n:
                width = max(1, n // 2)
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width)))
            p[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25)))
        ps.append(_normalize_positive(p))

    if hs_arr is not None and int(hs_arr.size) > 0:
        ps.append(_resample_p(hs_arr, n))

    return ps


def _try_inner(p, best_p, best_score):
    """Update internal best if p has lower internal score."""
    if p is None:
        return best_p, best_score
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    sc, _ = _score_p_with_c(p)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < float(best_score):
        return p, float(sc)
    return best_p, best_score


def _safe_evaluate(lst):
    """Call the judge evaluator defensively."""
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def _try_exact(p, best_list, best_exact):
    """Update exact best using evaluate_sequence."""
    if p is None:
        return best_list, best_exact
    lst = [float(float(x)) for x in p]
    sc = _safe_evaluate(lst)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < float(best_exact):
        return lst, float(sc)
    return best_list, best_exact


def _active_count(n):
    """Number of active LP constraints to use."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1100:
        return 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 2500:
        return 2048
    return 1600


def _n_weight(n):
    """Chunk time allocation for a length."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 64:
        return 60.0
    if n <= 128:
        return 90.0
    if n <= 256:
        return 150.0
    if n <= 512:
        return 240.0
    if n <= 1024:
        return 400.0
    if n <= 2048:
        return 350.0
    if n <= 4096:
        return 250.0
    return 220.0


def _improve_sequence(p, deadline, seed, active_count=1600):
    """Run local improvement on one length up to deadline; return best local."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    p = _normalize_positive(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    curr_score, c = _score_p_with_c(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = float(curr_score)

    beta = 5.0
    step = 0.25
    no_improve = 0
    lp_wait = 2.0 + float(n) / 500.0
    last_lp = time.monotonic() - lp_wait - 1.0
    accept_temp = 2e-4
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline):
        it += 1

        if time.monotonic() - last_lp >= lp_wait:
            last_lp = time.monotonic()
            g = _lp_direction(p, float(np.max(c)), active_count)

            if g is not None:
                # Slight blend to ensure support is everywhere.
                g = _normalize_positive(0.95 * g + 0.05 * p)
                new_p, new_s = _line_search(p, g, curr_score, alphas=_ALPHAS_LP)
                if new_s < curr_score - 1e-12:
                    p = new_p
                    curr_score = float(new_s)
                    c = _square_conv(p)
                    no_improve = 0
                    beta = max(1.0, beta * 0.9)
                    lp_wait = max(0.8, lp_wait * 0.9)
                    if curr_score < best_score:
                        best_score = curr_score
                        best_p = p.copy()
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
                    beta = min(60.0, beta * 1.08)
                    lp_wait = min(18.0, lp_wait * 1.15)
                    c = _square_conv(p)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                c = _square_conv(p)

        else:
            p2 = _multiplicative_step(
                p, c, beta=beta, step=step, rng=rng,
                noise=0.0 if no_improve < 20 else 0.02,
            )
            new_p, new_s = _line_search(p, p2, curr_score, alphas=_ALPHAS_CHEAP)

            accept = False
            if new_s < curr_score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif accept_temp > 1e-8 and new_s < curr_score + accept_temp:
                # Small simulated-annealing acceptance.
                if rng.random() < 0.2:
                    accept = True

            if accept:
                p = new_p
                curr_score = float(new_s)
                c = _square_conv(p)
                no_improve = 0
                accept_temp = max(1e-8, accept_temp * 0.97)
                if curr_score < best_score:
                    best_score = curr_score
                    best_p = p.copy()
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                beta = min(80.0, beta * 1.04)
                step = min(0.8, step * 1.02)
                accept_temp = max(1e-8, accept_temp * 0.95)

            # C is valid as long as p was not accepted.
            if (it % 7) == 0:
                c = _square_conv(p)

        if no_improve > 80:
            p = _perturb_p(best_p, rng, strength=0.15)
            curr_score, c = _score_p_with_c(p)
            if not (curr_score < best_score):
                p = best_p.copy()
                curr_score = best_score
                c = _square_conv(p)
            no_improve = 0
            beta = 8.0
            step = 0.3
            accept_temp = 5e-4

        if (it % 1000) == 0:
            print(
                f"    local n={n} it={it} curr={curr_score:.8f} best={best_score:.8f}",
                flush=True,
            )

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = float("inf")
    return best_p, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Search for a high-scoring (low value) nonnegative sequence within budget_s.

    Uses length-adaptive mirror descent and active-set LP directions,
    with periodic exact judge evaluations and a final refinement loop.
    """
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, budget_s - 25.0)

    # Candidate construction list.
    n_set = set([64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096])

    hs_arr = None
    try:
        hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if hs is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64)
            if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size > 0:
                arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
                if float(arr.sum()) > 0.0:
                    hs_arr = _normalize_positive(arr)
    except Exception:
        hs_arr = None

    if hs_arr is not None:
        hlen = int(hs_arr.size)
        if 32 <= hlen <= 5000:
            n_set.add(hlen)
        else:
            n_set.add(4096)

    all_n = [n for n in sorted(n_set) if 32 <= n <= 5000]
    if not all_n:
        all_n = [512]

    best_int_p = None
    best_int_score = np.inf
    best_list = None
    best_exact = np.inf
    last_exact = start - 100.0

    print(f"propose_candidate start: budget={budget_s:.1f}s target={_TARGET}", flush=True)

    # Initial candidates for all lengths.
    init_best = {}
    for n in all_n:
        inits = _initial_set(n, seed, hs_arr)
        bp_local = None
        bs_local = np.inf
        for p in inits:
            p, s = _try_inner(p, bp_local, bs_local)
            if bs_local != s or bp_local is None:
                bp_local, bs_local = p, s
        init_best[n] = (bp_local, float(bs_local))

        p, sc = _try_inner(bp_local, best_int_p, best_int_score)
        best_int_p, best_int_score = p, float(sc)

    if best_int_p is not None:
        best_list, best_exact = _try_exact(best_int_p, best_list, best_exact)
        last_exact = time.monotonic()

    print(f"initial internal best={best_int_score:.8f} exact best={best_exact:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Priorities: attack larger useful lengths first, while still trying several.
    order = []
    for n in [1024, 2048, 512, 4096, 256, 128, 64]:
        if n in all_n:
            order.append(n)
    if hs_arr is not None:
        hlen = int(hs_arr.size)
        if hlen in all_n and hlen not in order:
            # Put the supplied construction after the larger powers of two.
            inserted = False
            for i, x in enumerate(order):
                if x > 2048 and not inserted:
                    order.insert(i, hlen)
                    inserted = True
                    break
            if not inserted:
                order.append(hlen)
    for n in all_n:
        if n not in order:
            order.append(n)

    # Scheduled searches over lengths.
    for n in order:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 45.0:
            break

        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        chunk = min(remaining, _n_weight(n))
        if chunk < 30.0:
            continue

        p0, s0 = init_best.get(n, (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n), np.inf))

        if best_int_p is not None:
            p_best_resampled = _resample_p(best_int_p, n)
            p_best_resampled, s_best_res = _try_inner(p_best_resampled, p0, s0)
            p0, s0 = p_best_resampled, s_best_res

        if hs_arr is not None:
            ph = _resample_p(hs_arr, n)
            ph, sh = _try_inner(ph, p0, s0)
            p0, s0 = ph, sh

        active = _active_count(n)
        local_deadline = time.monotonic() + chunk

        print(
            f"scheduled n={n} chunk={chunk:.1f}s start_score={s0:.8f} "
            f"overall_best={best_int_score:.8f}",
            flush=True,
        )

        p_local, sc_local = _improve_sequence(p0, local_deadline, seed + 7919 * n + 1, active)

        p_after, sc_after = _try_inner(p_local, best_int_p, best_int_score)
        best_int_p, best_int_score = p_after, float(sc_after)

        if time.monotonic() - last_exact > 2.5:
            best_list, best_exact = _try_exact(best_int_p, best_list, best_exact)
            last_exact = time.monotonic()

        print(
            f"scheduled n={n} done internal_best={best_int_score:.8f} exact_best={best_exact:.8f}",
            flush=True,
        )

    # Final refinement on the best internal sequence found.
    final_stalls = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0 and best_int_p is not None:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        chunk = min(remaining, 300.0)
        if chunk < 30.0:
            break

        n_best = int(best_int_p.size)
        if n_best > 5000:
            best_int_p = _resample_p(best_int_p, 4096)
            n_best = 4096

        active = _active_count(n_best)
        p_local, sc_local = _improve_sequence(
            best_int_p, time.monotonic() + chunk, seed + 104729 * n_best + 17, active,
        )

        improved = False
        p_after, sc_after = _try_inner(p_local, best_int_p, best_int_score)
        if sc_after < best_int_score - 1e-12:
            best_int_p, best_int_score = p_after, float(sc_after)
            improved = True
            final_stalls = 0
        else:
            final_stalls += 1
            if final_stalls >= 4:
                break

        if time.monotonic() - last_exact > 2.5:
            best_list, best_exact = _try_exact(best_int_p, best_list, best_exact)
            last_exact = time.monotonic()

        print(
            f"final refine chunk={chunk:.1f}s improved={improved} "
            f"internal_best={best_int_score:.8f} exact_best={best_exact:.8f}",
            flush=True,
        )

    # Final exact check on the best internal sequence.
    if best_int_p is not None:
        best_list, best_exact = _try_exact(best_int_p, best_list, best_exact)

    # Fallback construction if the evaluator rejected everything.
    if best_list is None or not np.isfinite(best_exact):
        if best_int_p is not None:
            lst = [float(float(x)) for x in best_int_p]
            return lst
        n_fb = 512
        lst = [1.0 / float(n_fb) for _ in range(n_fb)]
        return lst

    return best_list
```
